Pockets Nook does not buy are greyed; A on one shows why at the bottom of the screen ("That's a delivery - Nook won't buy it", "Nook won't buy a wrapped present", "Bells can't be sold", "Nook doesn't buy turnips on Sunday", "Stationery can't be sold here", "Nook won't buy that"). **Controls (revised after user feedback: "it remembers where I put all the items ... I want it to NOT remember that, and B should bring up the 'Oh, you've changed your mind?' dialogue"):** the right part of the window is the **Sell** button (green with the count / total; grey and blocked while the basket is empty: "Put something in the basket first"); move the cursor right from the grid onto it and press **A**, or press **Start (Enter)** anywhere in the screen, to CONFIRM. Nook then quotes "Let me see here... That's N items in all. I'll give you X Bells for the lot. Deal?" (a free lot: "I can't pay anything for these, but I'll take them off your hands") with Yes. / No. Yes sells everything in one go and continues with the vanilla "anything else?"; No = the vanilla sell-cancel row + "anything else?". **B (Shift) / Y = CANCEL**: the basket is emptied (the items reappear in the pockets: they never left them), no quote, no sale, and Nook says his VANILLA sell-cancel row (`aNSC_MSG_SELL_CANCEL`, the row vanilla shows when you back out of the sell picker), then his vanilla "anything else?" choice. **Nothing is remembered:** every open starts with an EMPTY basket. A hint line at the bottom names the controls ("A: Add or Take Back    Sell: A on Sell or Start    B: Cancel"; the game font has no "/": it draws a note a refusal notice takes its place for ~3 s). Single player keeps the vanilla picker (incl. its X-mark "Sell all").

**Study (evidence):**
* Vanilla already knows a MIXED multi-item sale: in the sell picker (inventory mode 5, `mSM_IV_OPEN_SELL`) X marks several pockets (`mTG_mark_main_sub` `mTG_MARK_TYPE_INV_SELL_ITEM`) and "Sell all" (`mTG_sell_all_proc`) fills `submenu->item_p[]` with every marked (item, slot) and `selected_item_num = n`; `aNSC_check_buy_item_plural` / `aNSC_buy_check_init` (value = sum of price / 4, turnips Kabu price x bundle) / `aNSC_buy_check` (`selected_item_num > 1`: every slot -> empty or a 30,000 bag) sell them. The multiplayer client seam `aNSC_pc_sell_prepare` already turns that list into ONE SHOP_SELL slot mask, and the host's SHOP_SELL (TXN kind 11) already values EVERY masked slot of the pre-image itself (`pcnetgame_shop_sell_plan`, all or nothing, vanilla bag overflow, NO_ROOM, `mSP_PlusSales(value / 2)`, journal / replay). The shape check only requires the primary slot's bit (`aux_item & (1 << slot)`, bits 0..14). So the wire already carries mixed sales: **no new transaction kind, service, reason or message id** was needed (the planned kind 23 / service 11 were dropped: old hosts of protocol 8 answer the basket's sale exactly like a vanilla X-mark sale).
* What Nook refuses (vanilla): the picker's filter `m_submenu.c mSM_check_item_for_sell` (only NORMAL-condition pockets: no delivery / QUEST item, no wrapped PRESENT; no money bag, no exercise card, no knife and fork, no Wisp spirit); the dialogue refuses a QUEST item (`aNSC_CHECK_BUY_REFUSE_QUEST_COND`, `mTG_sell_proc` warning) and turnip bundles on Sunday (`aNSC_CHECK_BUY_NO_SUNDAY_TURNIPS`); a worthless item (price / 4 == 0, spoiled turnips) is taken for free ("take it off your hands"). The network seam additionally refuses stationery stacks (host NOT_SELLABLE since milestone 2). Selling has no sale-day multiplier and no shop-level factor (only buying does).
* Wallet cap: vanilla never refuses for a full wallet: `aNSC_check_money_overflow` + the sale turn every 30,000 over 99,999 into a 30,000 bag (sold slots first, then free pockets; MONEY_OVERFLOW row only when the bags do not fit). The host plan is the same (NO_ROOM).

**Design / code:**
* `pc/src/pc_sell_logic.c` / `.h` (pure, macros only): `pc_sell_refusal(item, cond, is_sunday)` = THE predicate (the vanilla filter + quest + Sunday turnips + stationery + unknown ids), `pc_sell_unit_value()` (price / 4, turnips, spoiled 0), the refusal texts, Bells formatting, the basket staging (`PCSellBasket`: cell -> pocket slot + item, next free cell, take back, the 15-bit mask, the selection in cell order, prune of pockets that changed). Native selftest `pc_sell_logic_selftest`.
* Host: `pcnetgame_shop_sell_unit` (pc_net_game.c) now calls `pc_sell_refusal` / `pc_sell_unit_value` with the host's prices and weekday (stricter than before for presents / money bags / cards / spirits / unknown ids: a legitimate client never offers those, the vanilla picker greys them).
* `pc/src/pc_sell_ui.c` / `.h`: the basket state and the Nook seam helpers; `pc_dresser_ui.c` BASKET mode (`pc_dresser_ui_begin_basket`, `pc_dresser_ui_basket`): the dresser screen's window, cells, icons, hand, tags and scripted test pad are reused; grid contents / take / put / hidden pockets delegate to the local staging (no host transaction, no Grab). `m_tag_ovl.c`: the 5 x 3 basket table (the left five dresser columns), "Add to Basket" / "Take Back" tags (the dresser's put / take procs), the refusal notice on a greyed pocket, no name tag on a staged pocket, cursor up limited to the basket columns. `m_haniwa_ovl.c`: 5 cells per row, the summary lines, the stale-basket guard (construct / destruct). `m_inventory_ovl.c`: the selectable (greyed) mask = the predicate. `pc_pause_menu.c`: the notice line (bottom of the screen).
* Nook seam (`ac_npc_shop_common.c`): `aNSC_buy_menu_close_wait_init` opens the inventory in HANIWA_ENTRUST mode + basket mode when `pc_sell_ui_enabled()` (else the vanilla mode 5); `aNSC_buy_menu_close_wait` collects the basket into the vanilla selection (`pc_sell_ui_close`); `aNSC_msg_win_open_wait` takes the quote row `PC_NOOK_MSG_SELL_BASKET` (pc_nook_house.c, appended: count 53; built without the "choice made" demo order) with counter = the count (plural path, also for ONE item: never the "all of these?" offer); `aNSC_buy_check` reads it like the vanilla multi-item offer (Yes = the unchanged vanilla local sale on the listen host / the unchanged network seam on a client: ONE SHOP_SELL); Yes and No call `pc_sell_ui_reset` (the old `pc_sell_ui_keep_next` "No keeps the basket" is gone).
* Cancel / confirm (the revision): `pc_sell_ui_close` hands Nook the basket ONLY after `pc_sell_ui_confirm()` (the Sell button / Start: `m_tag_ovl.c mTG_move_base` intercepts A on cursor column 5 or Start in basket mode and closes through the screen's own `mTG_move_cancel`; an empty basket is blocked); any other close (B / Y) writes an EMPTY selection (item EMPTY_NO, `selected_item_num` 0) = the vanilla "nothing selected" path (`aNSC_CHECK_BUY_NONE_SELECTED` -> `aNSC_MSG_SELL_CANCEL` 0x1098, Timmy / Tommy 0x171A -> `BUY_CONTINUE_CHECK`); no new Nook row (PC_NOOK_MSG_COUNT stays 53). The cursor table of the basket is 6 x 3 (`PC_SELL_TABLE_COLS`; column 5 of every row = the button at x 42; up from the pockets never lands on it); `pc_sell_table_cell()` (pure, native selftest) maps a table index to the cell (`pc_dresser_ui.c` basket branches). `m_haniwa_ovl.c` draws the button panel (green / grey) + "Sell" / count / total; `pc_pause_menu.c` the hint line. State cleared (`pc_sell_ui_reset`): every open, the cancel, the confirmed close (after the selection was built), Yes / No, the end of the conversation (`aNSC_request_Q_end_wait`), a scene change and a client link that is not READY (`pc_sell_ui_tick` in `pc_net_game_poll`), the stale-basket guard at the gyroid overlay construct.
* Conservation: nothing leaves the pockets while the basket is open (a view); a client's pockets / wallet change only by the host's APPLIED post-image, the listen host's by the vanilla local sale.
* Guests are full shop participants (unchanged host rule); the listen host's own player uses the same screen with the vanilla local sale (the host is authoritative for itself; the predicate is the same function).
* Switches: `settings.ini [Network] bulk_sell = 1` (default; 0 = the vanilla picker), env `AC_MP_BULK_SELL=0` (this process). Per process (UI only): the host's behaviour does not depend on it, so there is no host announcement / old-host latch.

**Tests:** `test_sell_basket_native.py` (the pure selftest: 32 predicate cases incl. every vanilla refusal, values, formatting, staging); `test_sell_basket_src.py` (26 source pins: one predicate on host + screen, no wire change, no pocket write before APPLIED, the Nook seam, the screen, switches, test hook gating); `test_sell_basket_protocol.py` (REAL listen host + FakeClients, 35 checks: the host values of fish / insect / apple / furniture / a stock item, ONE mixed sale of five = the sum, rev + 1, one committed line, sales_sum + value / 2, replay executed once, a money bag / wrapped present / exercise card / Wisp spirit / delivery inside the basket refuses the WHOLE sale with nothing changed, stale base / wrong primary / empty masked slot, the wallet-cap bag, two clients at once, a guest); `test_sell_basket_real.py` (REAL host + REAL client, Samsung display, volume 1): R1..R3 the basket screen opened by the hook, the real cursor / tag / B input of the scripted pad, ONE SHOP_SELL APPLIED, host value == the client's advisory total, final pockets (14/14, screenshot `sell_basket_open.png`); R4 the REAL Nook dialogue: first choice -> the basket, staging, the quote row, Yes -> ONE SHOP_SELL APPLIED (16/16, screenshots `sell_basket_nook.png`, `sell_basket_quote.png`). Updated pins: `test_shop_src.py` (H: the shared predicate), the PC message count 53 in `test_evnpc_shared_src.py`, `test_guest_mailbox_src.py`, `test_house_upgrade_src.py`, `test_map_line_src.py`; the native message harnesses (`test_work_menu_patch.py`, `test_dresser_msg_patch.py`) also link `pc_sell_logic.c`. Test-only aids: `AC_TEST_SELL_BASKET=1|2` (pc_sell_ui.c, PC_NET_TEST_HOOKS builds + `AC_TEST_HOOKS=1`), `g_pc_th_submenu_data0` (m_submenu.c test open with a data0).

**Tests of the controls revision:** `test_sell_basket_native.py` (+ the cursor-table mapping in the selftest); `test_sell_basket_src.py` 33 pins (new B1..B7: B = cancel with an empty selection, the vanilla sell-cancel path, every open empty, the reset points, the confirm intercept, the 6 x 3 table, the button + hint); `test_sell_basket_real.py` **SB2** (the new default; REAL host + REAL resident client, the real Nook dialogue, one talk sequence, 30/30 in two runs): open 1 stage 3 + the bag refused + B -> "CANCELLED (B)", Nook's vanilla row 0x1098 ("Ah, well. Is there anything else you wish to s[ell]..."), no SHOP_SELL; open 2 EMPTY again, A on the Sell button -> quote -> No -> state cleared, still no SHOP_SELL; open 3 EMPTY again, Start -> quote -> Yes -> exactly ONE SHOP_SELL mask 0x0007 APPLIED (host value 700 == advisory), final pockets. Frames: `sb2_sell_button.png` (the green "Sell / 3 items / 700 / Bells" button and the hint line, caught in the slide-out after the confirm), `sb2_cancel_row.png`, `sb2_quote.png` (the test grabs frames in game: TEST-ONLY `AC_TEST_FRAME_DUMP` in pc_main.c, since the python side reaches the pause steps late, the "staged" frame was taken after the close). The scripted pad gained the barrier step 1024 (pc_net_game.c, TEST-ONLY). Regression: `test_dresser_ui_source.py` 80/80, `test_shop_src.py` 65/65, `wire_baseline.py` 20/20, `test_work_menu_patch.py` 10/10, `test_sell_basket_protocol.py` 35/35.

**Not covered / known:** the listen host's own basket sale was not run in a real game (source-audited; the vanilla local plural sale is unchanged); the summary shows the CLIENT's advisory total (the host's value is authoritative and was equal in every run); the basket offers no Grab (the order of the cells does not matter for the sale).

## Emotes (radial wheel)

**Request:** an emote wheel like the tool wheel: F on the keyboard, hold D-pad Up on a controller (the right stick picks), hold-and-release; usable on foot anywhere (indoors and outdoors); the game's own marks above the head, visible to the other players; rebindable in the existing Keybindings menu, together with Chat and the tool wheel.

**Use:** hold **F** (keybindings.ini `Emote`) or **D-pad Up** (`Pad_Emote`), point at a slice (mouse relative to the window centre, or the right stick, latched; parking the stick in the centre for 0.3 s = nothing), release. Eight slices clockwise from the top: Hooray, Shocked, Surprised, Pray, Phew, Boo!, Twirl, Exercise (game-font symbols as icons). Only while standing / walking / running free (WAIT..DASH, no demo, on the ground, not in water; no menu, message, conversation, cutscene, pause, chat line, tool wheel; not the host observer). Hooray / Twirl / Exercise move the arms and need EMPTY HANDS (their slices are greyed with "Needs empty hands"; the tool is never stowed: that would change the inventory); the others keep the held tool's upper-body pose. 1 s cooldown. Any stick or button input stops an emote. Switches: `settings.ini [Gameplay] emotes = 1` (default), env `AC_EMOTES=0`.

**Emotes as implemented:**

| # | Emote | Clip(s) | Marks / sounds (local and on the other players' puppets) |
|---|-------|---------|-----------------------------------------------------------|
| 1 | Hooray | YATTA1 (128) 0.5 | SIAWASE_HANA flowers (continuous, killed at the end) |
| 2 | Shocked | the vanilla SHOCK state (GAAAN1 -> GAAAN2) | SHOCK lines (vanilla; the puppet uses the existing SHOCK row) |
| 3 | Surprised | BIKU1 (118) 0.5 | KANTANHU '!' at the start |
| 4 | Pray | OMAIRI_US1 (156) 0.5 | none |
| 5 | Phew | TIRED1 (97) 0.5, twice | ASE2 sweat (continuous), frame 10: DUST + the gendered araiiki breath; mouth 4 (face 0x44) |
| 6 | Boo! | ITAZURA1 (125) 0.5 | HALLOWEEN smoke at the start |
| 7 | Twirl | MENU_CHANGE1 (73) 1.0 | frame 16: wear sound + KIGAE sparkle |
| 8 | Exercise | TAISOU1 .. TAISOU7_2 (144-155), 0.5 (5_x 0.25), looping | none; cap 60 s |

Every clip emote is capped at 10 s (Exercise 60 s), timed on accumulated frame time (frame-rate independent).

**Design (no new player state, no new wire message):** an emote OVERLAY inside the vanilla WAIT state (`src/game/m_player_pc_emote.c_inc`, hooked into `m_player_main_wait.c_inc` under TARGET_PC): the clip is bound with the vanilla `Player_actor_SetupItem_Base1` + `Player_actor_InitAnimation_Base2` (STOP, morph -5), the face follows the clip's eye / mouth tables (blinking where a clip has none), the cues fire at keyframes; the rest of WAIT runs unchanged (any tool / A / B action or the stick leaves WAIT as usual, villagers can still start a conversation). `Player_actor_change_main_index` (PC hook next to `pc_net_game_note_player_main_entry`) ends the overlay on every state entry; a finished clip restores WAIT1 like `setup_main_Wait`. A choice made while walking waits up to 0.7 s for WAIT. Shocked requests `Player_actor_request_main_shock_all` (as the player-vs-player shovel hit). The vanilla PRAY / TIRED / RADIO_EXERCISE states were not used (PRAY ends in DEMO_WAIT and needs a shrine position, TIRED never leaves on its own, RADIO_EXERCISE follows the radio).

**Network:** while the overlay plays, the MOVE `action_state` and the ACTION_EXT sampler report the state as **DEMO_WAIT (74)**; the overlay bumps the entry counter at its start and its end, so (74, counter) is a fresh pair that ends with the emote. ACTION_EXT (96) already carries any clip < 157 for DEMO_WAIT; the emote id goes into `variant` (a real scripted DEMO_WAIT keeps 0) and Phew's constant face into `face` (new local sample field `face_set`; the message is unchanged). `msg_ok` and the host relay never look at `variant`, so older peers accept the rows and simply play the clip without the marks; peers older than ACTION_EXT see an idle stand. MOVE `move_state` keeps the real class (WAIT -> IDLE). Receivers: emote cue rows in `s_aext_cues` keyed on DEMO_WAIT + clip + `variant` (new field, 0 = any); continuous effects are killed with the puppet's own item_name when the row ends. Not masked in the train-station zone (x 2200 / z 820 +-120 in the town): a DEMO_WAIT there means "the arrival train stands at the station" to a joining guest (`pcarr_join_class`), so the emote is then local only.

**Keybindings menu:** the Controls > Keybindings page lists three new rows after the D-pad: **Tool Wheel** (keyboard G, pad LB), **Chat** (keyboard T; pad column `-`: the line is typed), **Emote Wheel** (keyboard F, pad D-pad Up). Conflicts swap as before; the one deliberate exception is the pad pair Emote Wheel / D-Pad Up, which may share D-pad Up (free play never reads D-pad Up, only choice menus do; the wheel zeroes the buttons while it is open). keybindings.ini gains `Emote` / `Pad_Emote` (an old file keeps the defaults).

**Code:** `pc/include/pc_emote.h` + `pc/src/pc_emote.c` (pure: table, picking, gate, sequencer, wire masking; native selftest), `pc/src/pc_emote_wheel.c` (UI, input, kill switch, TEST-ONLY script `AC_TEST_EMOTE_SCRIPT=all`), `src/game/m_player_pc_emote.c_inc` (the overlay), small hooks in `m_player.c`, `m_player_main_wait.c_inc`, `pc_pad.c`, `graph.c`, `pc_tool_wheel.c`, `pc_chat_ui.c`, `pc_net_game.c` (EMOTE WHEEL block), `pc_remote_player.c` (cue rows), `pc_action_ext.*` (`face_set`), `pc_keybindings.*`, `pc_settings_menu.c`, `pc_settings.*`.

**Tests:** `emote_selftest.c` (256 checks), `test_emote_src.py` (source audit + the selftest), `action_ext_selftest.c` (+DEMO_WAIT with variant / face), `test_emote_real.py` (REAL listen host + 2 clients: every emote through the real wheel path, ACTION_EXT sends with variant / face, both observers apply the rows and fire the marks / sounds and kill the continuous effects, Shocked through the SHOCK row, Exercise cancelled by a stick push, cooldown / talk / chat gates refuse, inventory hash unchanged; screenshots `emote_A_wheel.png`, `emote_host_view.png`).

**Not covered / known:** the controller opener (D-pad Up + right stick) and the mouse picking were not driven by a real device in the test (the script forces the hold and the selection; the picking is native-tested); the submenu / water / full-body-with-tool refusals are native / source tested only; the ring is drawn in the 320x240 UI space like the tool wheel, so it looks horizontally stretched on a 16:9 window.

## Hand-over animations

**User request:** when Tom Nook (Work Mode) or a villager gives the player an item or takes one, the vanilla hand-over animation must play (the player's receive / give states with the item, the NPC's own give / take clips), for the player and for everybody watching.

**Vanilla (decomp):** one item actor does every hand-over: `ac_handOverItem` (`aHOI_CLIP`: `birth_proc(item, after_mode, present, master)` needs the talk actors of `mDemo` and locks the message window; `chg_request_mode_proc` TRANSFER -> TRANS_WAIT -> GET_PULL -> PUTAWAY; `chg_master_proc` when the receiver grabs it; the item shrinks away and the message is unlocked). NPC -> player: the talk script sets the demo order (`aQMgr_talk_common_set_npc_takeout`: NPC0[3] = 2 + NPC1 item / after mode / present) -> `aNPC_check_timing_demoCode` -> `aNPC_ACT_TRANS` (TRANSFER1 -> TRANS_WAIT1, the item held out) -> at TRANS_WAIT the item actor puts the player into RECIEVE_WAIT 66 -> RECIEVE_STRETCH 67 (GET1 / GET_F1 / RETURN_MAIL1) -> RECIEVE 68 -> RECIEVE_PUTAWAY 69. Player -> NPC: `mPlib_request_main_give_type1` (GIVE 70: TRANSFER1 + GASAGOSO, the item actor born with the player as master) -> GIVE_WAIT 71 sets TRANS_WAIT -> the script orders NPC0[3] = 3 (`aQMgr_set_npc_putaway`) -> `aNPC_ACT_GET` (GET1 -> GET_PULL1 -> GET_PUTAWAY1). The pocket write is the script's own, separate from the animation (the animation is presentation only). Nook's shop actor has its own copies (`aNSC_present_balloon_trans_*`, `aNSC_pc_present_*`).

**Multiplayer before:** a client's villager runs its vanilla actions during a conversation (the talk lease), so the vanilla quest flows (gifts, trades, errands, "bring me X", the give menu) already animated LOCALLY. Missing: (1) Nook Work Mode (Nook's rows TAKE_PARCEL / DELIVER) and the Work Mode villager talk (VILLAGER_GIVE / VILLAGER_RECEIVE) were host transactions with no animation at all; (2) the other players never saw the NPC side (the host holds a client-talked villager still, so NPC_MOVE carried WAIT / WALK; Nook is a per-process actor). The player side reached them through ACTION_EXT only when the real states ran.

**Now (ON by default; kill switch `AC_MP_HANDOVER_ANIM=0` or settings.ini `[Gameplay] handover_anim = 0`):**
- **Work Mode, Nook** (`ac_npc_shop_common.c`, `aNSC_pc_ho_*`, state `aNSC_PC_HO_RUN`): after the host's APPLIED (or the host's own player's local commit) of TAKE_PARCEL, Nook takes the parcel out (`birth_proc` with Nook as master, TRANSFER1 -> TRANS_WAIT1, GASAGOSO) and the player's vanilla 66-69 take it; of DELIVER, the player holds the item out (`mPlib_request_main_give_type1`: 70 / 71) and Nook takes it (GET1 -> `chg_master_proc` -> GET_PULL1 -> GET_PUTAWAY1). The job row waits at its end (message locked); the closing row follows the hand-over. REPORT pays Bells into the wallet (no item, no animation, like vanilla Nook's payments).
- **Work Mode, villager** (`ac_quest_talk_work.c_inc`, step `aQMgr_WORK_STEP_HAND`): VILLAGER_GIVE = the quest manager's own takeout order (the villager's `aNPC_ACT_TRANS`, the player's 66-69); VILLAGER_RECEIVE = the player gives the parcel (70 / 71) and the villager takes it (`aQMgr_set_npc_putaway` -> `aNPC_ACT_GET`), then an ITEM tip comes back the same way (a Bells tip goes to the wallet). The hello waits at its end; then the generated row as before.
- **Ordering / safety:** the plan comes from `pc_ho_work_plan(op, applied, ...)` (pure, `pc/include/pc_handover.h`): a refusal plays nothing. The pockets are the host's post-image, applied by the transaction core at APPLIED exactly as before (the dialogue hides the inventory, so the item is in the pocket by the time it can be seen). Every step has a time cap (start 4 s, whole 15 s; Nook 4-10 s per step) after which the message is unlocked and the clip restored, so the dialogue never hangs. Nothing in the new code writes a pocket, a condition, the wallet or a job (source-audited).
- **Other players, the NPC:** new message **103 HANDOVER** (RELIABLE, 16 B `PCHandoverMsg`: kind NPC_GIVES / NPC_TAKES, class VILLAGER / SHOP, slot, npc_id, item, origin, flags bit0 fish, seq, rsv). VILLAGER: every town villager's vanilla `aNPC_ACT_TRANS` / `aNPC_ACT_GET` start on a READY client (any talk: Work Mode, gifts, trades, quests, the give menu) is reported to the host (client -> host only). The host checks READY + shape + rate (4 per 2 s per peer) + that the sender holds that villager's conversation (talk-hold peer set + npc_id; the slot's npc_id), plays the matching action types on ITS talk-held villager (`aNPC_pc_host_handover_tick`: TRANSFER(_F) -> TRANSFER(_F)_WAIT, or GET -> GET_PULL -> GET_PUTAWAY (_F)) and then restores the previous action; the existing NPC_MOVE `action_type` shows it to every other client (their consumer villager plays the clips through `aNPC_setupAction`). Indoors (ROOM_NPC: the talker's own simulation is the pose stream) the other occupants now let a hand-over action of that talk through instead of forcing WAIT. SHOP: owner -> host -> every OTHER READY peer of the owner's announced scene (origin stamped); there an idle keeper with that npc_id (wait / walking after its player / turn) stops, plays the same steps once and resumes its action. Old peers drop 103 silently (no branch; outside the violation-counting ranges).
- **Other players, the player:** ACTION_EXT (96) already carries 67-71 with the item as `aux`; the real states now run, so the observers' puppets play GET1 / TRANSFER1 / TRANS_WAIT1 etc. as dynamic rows (verified in the real test).
- **Listen host's own player:** its Work Mode ops complete locally (`r == 3`) and take the same animation path; its own villager hand-overs are vanilla and reach the clients through NPC_MOVE; its Nook hand-overs are relayed to the clients in the shop.

**Not covered:** the exchanged item is not drawn in a PUPPET's hand (ACTION_EXT `aux` carries it; the puppet has no left-arm item path yet) nor in an observer's NPC hand (the item actor exists only on the owner's process); a villager the host does not simulate (stale fallback / relay modes) shows its hand-over to the others only through the relay owner's pose stream; Gulliver / K.K. / Wendell etc. (event NPC claims) keep their own flows; the shop counter flows (buy / sell / basket) and mail presents have no hand-over in vanilla either; an observer's keeper that is itself talking to its own player skips the relayed animation (and an idle keeper ignores the local player's A for the ~2 s it plays).

**Wire:** id 103 (v8 extended in place, no version bump); pinned in `wire_baseline.py`, `test_old_host_compat_src.py` (new ids), `test_pvp_src.py` (ids above 92), `test_handover_src.py`.

**Tests:** `handover_selftest.c` (42 checks: shape, script sequencing / caps / TTL, rate incl. the ms wrap, the Work Mode plan incl. refusal = nothing, the waiter); `test_handover_src.py` (38: wire, dispatch, routing / validation, the seams start the vanilla machinery only from APPLIED, unlock + timeouts, no pocket / wallet writes, kill switch, the selftest); `test_handover_protocol.py` (19, a real host + FakeClients: the held villager's NPC_MOVE action goes TRANSFER -> TRANSFER_WAIT -> back and GET -> GET_PULL -> GET_PUTAWAY -> back, a non-holder / an unknown villager refused, 8 malformed dropped, rate 4 of 8, the shop relay reaches the same scene only with the origin stamped, nobody gets a villager 103); `test_handover_real.py` (25, REAL listen host + worker A + observer B in Nook's shop, Samsung, volume 1, the autopilot `mashw`: DELIVER = A's GIVE 70 with the town fruit + GIVE_WAIT 71 and Nook's GET steps; TAKE_PARCEL = Nook's TRANSFER and A's RECIEVE 67 / 68 with the parcel + 69; both closing rows after the hand-overs; host commits ENTER x2 / DELIVER x1 / TAKE_PARCEL x1; B's keeper played GET_PULL / GET_PUTAWAY / done and TRANS_WAIT / done; B's puppet of A played 67-71 as ACTION_EXT rows). Test-only hooks: autopilot `mashw` (Nook's first page -> the Work Mode choice), `AC_TEST_HANDOVER_FRUIT=1` (the town fruit into a free pocket). **Not run on real processes:** the Work Mode villager talk (VILLAGER_GIVE / VILLAGER_RECEIVE + item tip) and a vanilla villager gift / trade seen by an observer (the host side is protocol-tested, the client seam source-audited).

## Roommates / move-in

**User request:** "if a guest walks up to a resident's gyroid, they can request to move in with that resident and become a resident themselves. they can share the house but the original buyer is the main house holder. the main house resident can go to his gyroid and see move-in requests and accept or deny them from there. they also later can remove them from the house, basically demoting them back into a guest." The gyroid is a gyroid PLACED OUTSIDE (outdoor furniture). A roommate keeps its own pockets / wallet / identity; only the house is shared.

**Status: implemented, not committed** (see "Evidence" below for what ran; BLOCKED: manual verification required).

### Investigation: can the 4 resident slots be raised? (decision: NO, the roommate is IMITATED)

What a "resident" is in this code base, read from the source:

| part | where | bound to the 4 slots |
|---|---|---|
| identity, pockets, wallet, bank, letters, catalog, quests | `Save_t.private_data[PLAYER_NUM]` (`include/m_common_data.h`: `private_data[4]` at 0x20, a fixed layout of the GCI) | yes, `PLAYER_NUM 4` (`m_personal_id.h`) |
| house (furniture, floors, mailbox, size / loan state) | `homes[PLAYER_NUM]` at 0x9CE8 of the same fixed layout | yes |
| the 4 house plots / door structures | the town map (`HOUSE0..3` structure tiles, `aMHS_*`) | yes, 4 physical houses |
| the save gyroid outside each house, the arrangement | `house_arrangement` is a `u8` holding 4 x 2 bits (`mHS_get_arrange_idx`; for `player_no 4` it returns house 0) | yes |
| villager memories, contest quests, fish records, museum donors | `PersonalID` keyed but resolved through `mPr_GetPrivateIdx` (0..3), donor nibbles 0..3 + 5 | yes |
| mail / post office | addressed by PersonalID, delivered only to a house holder (`mPO_receipt_check_mail`, `mMl_hunt_for_send_address`) | yes |
| multiplayer | `s_rec_slot[0..3]` resident lineages, `members.dat` resident credentials, the admission resolver (RESIDENT = one of the 4), `pcnetgame_mbox_house_of` | yes |

`PLAYER_NUM` has ~200 uses in `src/` + `include/`; the save image is the vanilla GCI layout every client and host reads. Raising the slot count would be a save-format break of the whole town (every client, every tool, the town transfer, records.dat, members.dat) for a feature that does not need a slot. **Option (B) "really raise slots" is rejected as not robust.** Option (A) is cheap and robust: a roommate STAYS a guest (`.gst` record, guest key, guest token, player_no 4 on its client) and gains rights over ONE resident's house through a host-side relation. The resident (the buyer) stays the only holder: only it upgrades the house (Nook's flows check the house owner), only it edits the furniture, only it gets house mail.

Facts found on the way that shaped the design:
* **A guest could always walk into any house.** The docs said "the vanilla foreigner branch makes the doors knock only"; the code (`m_player_common.c_inc Player_actor_Check_NecessaryDoorKnock` + `m_player_main_knock_door.c_inc`) only plays a KNOCK before the normal door entry. Inside, a guest is a visitor (`set_field_type` gives `mFI_FIELDTYPE2_ROOM`, never `PLAYER_ROOM`), so it cannot edit anything; but it saw ITS OWN (stale) local copy of the room, because the host never pushed a house to a guest (`pcnetgame_house_host_tick`: "a guest never sees (or edits) a house").
* Room editing (furniture move / pickup, the dresser's host storage, the music box) is owned by `field_type == PLAYER_ROOM` on the vanilla side and by `idx < PLAYER_NUM` + `mbox_house_of(pid)` + per-record conservation on the host (`pcnetgame_house_process_commit`). Two editors of one house would race on `s_hh[h].seq` (STALE + rollback pushes) and the conservation pair is one house + ONE record. **Shared editing is therefore NOT part of this change** (documented limit; it would need a vanilla owner override in `set_field_type`, the music box / dresser `player_no` paths redirected to the scene house, and a two-record conservation on the host).
* The placed gyroid already records its placer (`PCOfurnMarker.placer_kind / placer_key / placer_name`, C1); PLACE is residents-only, so the placer is a resident (kind RESIDENT) or the listen host's own player (kind HOST with the host resident's key). The client never gets the placer (98 / 102 carry tile, item, time only). Gyroids are `FTR_HNW_COMMON000..126` (`IS_ITEM_HANIWA`, 0x15B0..0x17AB), all 1x1.

### Design

**Who holds a gyroid.** The holder of a placed gyroid is its placer when that placer key is a resident of the town that owns a house (`homes[h].ownerID`). **Old pieces / no usable placer** (a test seed, a console placement, a resident that was deleted or has no house): the FIRST resident with a house that opens the gyroid's menu claims it (the marker's placer fields are rewritten to that resident, kind RESIDENT, and the sidecar saved; logged `claimed`); the operator can set it with the console `roommates owner <x> <z> <resident slot|name>`. No sidecar format change (the placer fields already exist in v2; `flags` stays 0, so a C3 build still reads the file).

**Relations: host sidecar `roommates.ini`** (next to `ops.ini`: `servers/<id>/` or `save/mp/`; `pc/src/pc_roommates.c`, pure, native selftest): versioned text, tolerant parser, atomic write, the previous generation kept as `roommates.ini.bak` (and read when the main file is missing after a crash between the two renames). One line per relation: state (1 pending, 2 roommate, 3 denied, 4 removed), holder key, guest key (the 20-byte canonical keys of ops.ini), time, a notice waiting for the guest, a notice waiting for the holder, both names (raw bytes, hex). DENIED / REMOVED are tombstones: they carry the offline notice and a 10-minute re-request cooldown for that pair and are dropped once both are spent. An unreadable file (I/O error, > 256 KB) makes the host refuse every change (BUSY) and never overwrite it.

**Rules (host, pure).** One open request per guest (any holder); a roommate of one house cannot ask elsewhere; at most 4 roommates and 4 open requests per holder; one request per guest every 15 s (memory) plus the 10-minute cooldown after a deny / removal (persisted); a resident cannot ask (it has a house: NOT_GUEST); the requester must stand next to the gyroid in the town (the host's own copy of its position, 2 tiles). Message flood guard: 8 messages per 2 s per peer, the rest dropped.

**Housekeeping (host, every 5 s, only while the guest store is TRUSTED and loaded).** A relation whose holder is no longer a resident with a house: roommates -> REMOVED / requests -> DENIED, with the notice "house gone" for the guest. A relation whose guest is no longer in this town's guest table (removed by the operator, promoted to a resident by a house purchase / `promote`, or a changed `guest.ini` identity = a NEW guest key): deleted. A changed guest identity is a new guest, exactly as for the guest store itself.

**Wire (protocol v8 extended in place, no version bump): message 104 ROOMMATE** (`PCRoomMsg`, `pc/include/pc_roommates.h`, fixed 300 bytes, RELIABLE). Client -> host: QUERY / REQUEST / CANCEL / ACCEPT / DENY / REMOVE / LEAVE, each naming the gyroid (origin tile + the tile value the client sees; ACCEPT / DENY / REMOVE carry the guest key in e[0]). Host -> client: INFO (the requester's role at that gyroid: holder (with the up to 8 requests + roommates, online flag), may ask, asked (may withdraw), roommate (may move out), blocked (+ why), another resident, nobody), RESULT (op + `PC_ROOM_R_*`), STATUS (pushed at READY and on every change: "you are a roommate of house h, holder X" or "not a roommate"), NOTICE (the other party's decision; a holder's "N move-in requests are waiting at your gyroid"). No new TXN kind, no new reason code, no new town service. **Capability:** bit 1 `PC_OFURN_C4_ROOMMATES` of the 102 CAPS `total` field (bit 0 is C3 storage; a C2 / C3 client ignores higher bits). A client sends 104 only after that bit; an old host never sees 104. An old client ignores 104 (the dispatcher's "unrecognized: ignore"). A malformed 104 is dropped and logged, never a violation.

**Offline parties.** A request to a holder that is offline is stored; the holder gets the NOTICE at its next join and sees the list at the gyroid. A decision while the guest is offline is stored as the guest's notice and delivered (with the STATUS) at its next join. Notices are cleared when sent (a link lost in the same instant can lose that one line; the state itself is never lost).

**What a roommate gets (client + host):**
* the holder's house door opens WITHOUT the knock (like the holder's own door; `Player_actor_Check_NecessaryDoorKnock` TARGET_PC hook);
* the LIVE room: the host pushes house h (only h) to that guest through the existing visitor path (CANON_PUSH, the client's visitor apply / live rebuild), so it sees the holder's real furniture instead of a stale copy (`pcnetgame_house_host_tick` guest gate relaxed for roommates, `pcnetgame_house_pump_push` per-peer house mask);
* resident rights for OUTDOOR furniture (place / grab / move / rotate / pick up, PERMS 0x0B like a resident; `pcnetgame_ofurn_peer_is_resident` counts a roommate);
* a "Welcome home" notice when it enters the shared house.

**What a non-roommate guest loses** (only on a host that has roommates ON): entering a resident's house. Pressing A at a house door that is not its shared house shows "Only the people who live here can go in" and nothing opens (`aMHS_check_player_sub` TARGET_PC hook). Residents keep visiting each other's houses (vanilla). On an old host (no capability bit) a guest behaves exactly as before.

**Demotion.** The holder's Remove (or the roommate's own "Move out") ends the relation: STATUS "not a roommate" -> a guest client standing in that house is shown out to the front of the door (the shop-closure pattern: no wipe, no demo / message / menu, then `goto_other_scene` with the exit door data the house entry wrote, with the vanilla walk-out music handling), the house pushes stop, the outdoor-furniture rights drop back to a guest's (PERMS 0x08). Pockets, wallet, record and letters are untouched (they were always the guest's own).

**Not shared (decisions):** furniture editing, the indoor dresser's host storage and the music box stay the holder's (see the investigation; the roommate can still open the outdoor storage like any guest). **Mail stays at the post office** for the roommate (the house mailbox is addressed to the holder's PersonalID and `mPO_receipt_check_mail` delivers only to a house holder; sharing it would need a second recipient key in the mailbox path of the host, the client view and the take transaction -- too much for this change). Nook's house upgrades / loan: the holder only (unchanged). The save gyroid next to the house door: vanilla (it serves only the house holder).

**UI.** A gyroid placed outside: a guest presses **A** in front of it (a guest has no grab); a resident TAPS A (a hold is still the C2 grab). The client asks the host (QUERY; "Asking..." after 0.5 s, gives up after 3 s with "Try again in a moment") and opens a small PC overlay menu (`pc/src/pc_room_ui.c`: title + up to 9 rows, D-pad / stick up-down, A select, B back; Yes/No confirm for every decision; the player stands still while it is open, the pad is consumed). Guest: "Ask to move in" (confirm "Ask <holder> if you can move in?"), "Withdraw my request", "Move out" (roommate), or the reason it cannot ask. Holder: "Accept <name>" / "Deny <name>" per request, "Remove <name>" per roommate (with "(online)"), "Close". The result shows as a notice line (y 94 in `pc_net_notice_draw`) and the holder's list refreshes. The listen host's own player can be a holder: its menu talks to the host logic directly (no message).

**Switches.** Host: `settings.ini [Network] roommates = 1` (default ON), env `AC_MP_ROOMMATES=0` (this process; host: no capability bit, 104 answered OFF; client: no menu). The door gate and the knock skip follow the host's bit.

**Console** (`--dedicated`): `roommates` (list: holder, guest, state, time, waiting notices), `roommates remove <guest name> confirm` (demote), `roommates clear <guest name> confirm` (delete every relation of that guest), `roommates owner <x> <z> <resident slot|name>` (set the holder of a placed gyroid).

### As built (deviations from the design above)

* The menu re-asks the host up to twice (700 ms apart) when its first QUERY is answered NOT_GYROID: the host's copy of a player that just stepped up to the gyroid can lag (seen in the real run).
* The show-out waits for an idle player (no message window, no submenu, `mPlib_able_submenu_type1`); a demo state holds it at most 10 s (`PCNG_ROOM_EJECT_DEMO_MS`), so a stuck demo flag never keeps a demoted guest inside. (The real `--guest` client of the test reported `mDemo_CheckDemo() == 1` the whole time it stood in the town; inside the house it did not.)
* The STATUS line of the client logs both holder keys and whether the local town's house matches (`the local town's house h holder matches / DIFFERS`): the door rule uses the vanilla `mPr_CheckCmpPersonalID` against the holder the host named.
* A request is answered by the table's own reason first (ALREADY / ROOMMATE_ELSEWHERE / ...); the 15 s gap only stops a NEW request (COOLDOWN).
* Files: `pc/include/pc_roommates.h` + `pc/src/pc_roommates.c` (pure: rules, roommates.ini, the 104 layout + validator, selftest), `pc/include/pc_room_ui.h` + `pc/src/pc_room_ui.c` (the menu + notice), `pc/src/pc_net_game_roommates.c_inc` (host / client / console / TEST-ONLY hooks, included once at the end of `pc_net_game.c`), small hooks in `pc_net_game.c` (enum 104, prototypes, the two ticks, the two dispatch branches, the house-push gate + per-peer house mask, the CAPS bit, the roommate's outdoor rights), `pc_ofurn_draw.c` (A at a gyroid), `pc_pad.c`, `src/graph.c`, `pc_pause_menu.c` (notice y 94), `src/actor/ac_my_house_move.c_inc` + `src/game/m_player_common.c_inc` (TARGET_PC door / knock hooks), `pc_dedicated.c` (`roommates`), `pc_settings.c/.h` (`roommates`), `pc_outdoor_furniture.h` (`PC_OFURN_C4_ROOMMATES`, the console prototype), `pc/CMakeLists.txt`.

### Known limits / not verified

* NOT seen by a person: the menu, the notices, the door that stays shut, the walk-in without the knock (the knock skip and the shut door are source-audited; the real test warps into the house instead of walking through the door).
* Shared editing is not offered: the roommate cannot move / pick up furniture, use the dresser's extra storage or the music box inside the shared house (owner-only on the vanilla side and the host); mail stays at the post office. Nook upgrades stay the holder's.
* The door rule is client-side (the host cannot stop a scene change): a modified / older client of a guest can still walk into any house as a visitor (it then sees its stale local copy; the host never pushes it a house it does not share).
* A notice is cleared when it is sent: a link lost in that instant loses that one line (never the relation).
* A guest whose guest entry disappears (operator `guest-remove`, a promotion, a changed `guest.ini` identity) loses its relations at the next housekeeping (5 s) while the guest store is trusted; if the host dies before the guest store wrote a NEW guest's file, that guest's relations are dropped at the next start (a relation without its guest is meaningless).
* Holder rows show at most 8 entries (4 requests + 4 roommates by construction). The listen host's own player can be a holder through the same menu (the local path: no message); its decision is applied by the host logic directly (not exercised by a test).
* `pc/build64_w3` still configures `PC_DRESSER_STORAGE_DEFAULT_ON=OFF` (unrelated to roommates; outdoor storage tests set `AC_MP_DRESSER_STORAGE=1`).
